## Decisions applied
| DEFAULT / ADR | What | Source | Override / status |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADR-REG-001 | RPT owns the Check run, findings and Check Document rows; CHK owns none | P0 (REG) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-REG-002 | CHK declares the result port; RPT implements it | P0 (REG) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-REG-003 | Versions immutable; a new Check uses the current version | P0 (REG) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-REG-006 | Check limits are platform configuration | P0 (REG) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-DOC-001 | DOC runs the document source query in every fetch mode | P0 (DOC) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-DOC-008 | End-of-Check notice on every ending path | P1 (DOC) | ACCEPTED by owner |
| ADR-DOC-009 | Free-tier rule from model tier + environment data class | P1 (DOC) | ACCEPTED by owner — aligned by ADR-CHK-010 |
| ADR-CHK-001 | CHK masters Check status, finding outcome, failure reason; carries Overall Status by value | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-002 | SATISFIED / NOT_SATISFIED / UNDETERMINED; NOT_COMPLIANT over NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW over COMPLIANT | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-003 | Explicit values and dates recomputed in code; unverifiable evidence → UNDETERMINED | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-004 | `manual` Check waits in AWAITING_DOCUMENTS within the upload window | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-005 | Unread query data reported; failures end FAILED with a reason; start-up closes unfinished Checks | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-006 | Free-tier rule for the comparison model | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-007 | Independent Checks per request; version pinned at start | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-008 | Start needs service code, request number, employee identity; refused if unavailable | P0 | Confirmed at prd-approval |
| ADR-CHK-009 | Every §12 guardrail stated at CHK's surface | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-010 | Environment data class is one platform configuration value shared with DOC; comparison model tier is CHK's model configuration | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-011 | Check result port operations; CHK owns no report entity (narrowed by ADR-CHK-015); working data in memory per Check | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-012 | Known-result request set as a model-evaluation run; go-live switch operational | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-013 | In-process start / confirm operations with typed rejections; no CHK HTTP operation at P1; notice retried once | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-014 | Required document type SATISFIED with one READ outcome; unread query entries as text | P1 (this stage) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| ADR-CHK-015 | Transient Active Check entity: one row per unfinished Check with status and deadline; exactly-once ending | P1 (this stage, revision) | ACCEPTED — non-breaking |
| DEFAULT — comparison model tier FREE when not declared | An undeclared comparison model tier counts as FREE | ADR-CHK-006; domain-profile G13 | Override: declare APPROVED after the D3 go-live gate |
| DEFAULT — data class REAL when not declared | An undeclared environment data class counts as REAL | ADR-CHK-010, ADR-DOC-009 | Override: declare SYNTHETIC in test environments |
| DEFAULT — upload window measured from the Check's creation | The upload window counts from the moment the `manual` Check is created | ADR-CHK-004 | Override: count from the last upload |
| DEFAULT — one retry of the end-of-Check notice | A failed notice is sent once more, then logged | ADR-CHK-013; domain best practice | Override: more retries with back-off |

## Access summary
| Role | Screens | Operations |
|---|---|---|
| Employee | none in CHK (embedded frontend through INT) | start a Check (from the host, via INT); confirm the uploads of a `manual` Check (via INT); reads the report from RPT via INT |
| Service Administrator | none | configures service packages and connections (REG), the comparison model and the platform configuration (limits, upload window, data class) |
| INT, RPT (in-process) | — | INT: start a Check, confirm uploads · RPT: implements the Check result port |
Caller authentication is deferred (raw-idea A2); no role check is specified in this version.

# UI/UX SPEC — Check Engine (CHK)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Module : CHK   Version : v1   Profile : aias   Stage : P3.2 (Part A — UX design)
Inputs : srs-chk.md (SCR-REQ 0) · prd-chk.md · api-spec-chk.yaml · registry-srs-chk.md · registry-exec-be-chk.md
Counts : SCR 0 · UXD 0   ADRs : ADR-CHK-019
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## Screens

No `SCR-*` block. The SRS of CHK declares no screen entry (PART B not applicable; module-registry
AUTO-DECISION; [KB:raw-idea.md §15 A1]), and this stage mints one `SCR-*` per SRS screen entry and
nothing more (§A.1). The employee frontend of amendment A1 is owned by Host Integration (INT), which
holds all five screens (SCR-REQ-INT-001 … SCR-REQ-INT-005) and their fields, compositions and states.

## Cross-module display dependencies

No `UXD-*`. A UXD is keyed by the module that owns the screen that displays foreign data (§A.5). CHK
owns no screen, so it has no UXD to mint. If one of INT's screens renders the Active Check read by
API-CHK-001, that screen's module (INT) mints the UXD (ADR-CHK-019).

## Frontend surface of CHK (consumed, not displayed by CHK)

What the frontend can read from CHK, stated as design intent for Part B. The fields come from the SRS
(ENT-CHK-001) and the shapes from api-spec-chk.yaml:

| Field (ENT-CHK-001) | Label en | Label ar | Read-only | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| checkId | Check | PENDING ADR-CHK-018 | yes | API-CHK-001 response |
| checkStatus (CHECK_STATUS: AWAITING_DOCUMENTS · RUNNING) | Status (Awaiting documents · Running) | PENDING ADR-CHK-018 | yes | API-CHK-001 response |
| deadlineAt | Deadline | PENDING ADR-CHK-018 | yes | API-CHK-001 response |

- Meaning of the deadline (REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077): the end of the upload window while AWAITING_DOCUMENTS,
  and the end of the Check timeout (counted from RUNNING) while RUNNING.
- A Check that has ended (COMPLETED or FAILED) has no Active Check (REQ-CHK-078). The read then answers "not
  found", and a consumer shows the ended Check from the Report Store record that INT reads. It does not
  show a refusal.
- Permissions: none. There is no permission model; caller authentication is deferred (raw-idea A2).
- No input, no submit: CHK takes nothing from the employee through the frontend. Starting a Check and
  confirming uploads are INT's endpoints, which call CHK in-process (ADR-CHK-013, ADR-CHK-017).

Labels are copied from the SRS (A3 field labels, A6 CHECK_STATUS labels). Arabic labels are PENDING ADR-CHK-018,
because the SRS gives no Arabic text and none is translated here.

## RECONCILIATION — CHK v1

```
RECONCILIATION — CHK v1
B1 every US-* used in a flow has an SRS counterpart (REQ/AC/screen)   → 0 flows (flow-diagram-chk.md); nothing excluded
B2 no RULE-* contradicts a flow/spec outcome                           → 0 SCR blocks; the consumed surface agrees with RULE-CHK-009 (unfinished statuses only)
B3 every field/permission on a screen exists in the SRS               → 0 screens; the three consumed fields are ENT-CHK-001's, no permission added
B4 every screen entry of the SRS has exactly one SCR-* block          → 0 SRS screen entries · 0 SCR-* blocks
RESULT  reconciled 0 · reworked 0 · ADRs ADR-CHK-019
```

<<<END ARTIFACT>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-001.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-001 — CHK masters the closed lists it produces — Check status, finding outcome and Check failure reason — and carries them and the profile's Overall Status by value through its result port
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 6c3d3eb93f7b
traces      : POL-CHK-015, POL-CHK-017, POL-CHK-019, POL-CHK-020

## Decision
The raw idea names the Overall Status values (§7), a Check status a host polls (§5, §8 "Return the status") and a CHECK_RUN row holding both "status" and "result" (§9). The domain-profile maps the terms Overall Status and Finding to RPT vocabulary, but the values are produced by CHK, and RPT depends on CHK (owner graph; ADR-REG-002), so CHK cannot read a list RPT masters without a cycle. Options: A) RPT masters the lists — needs CHK → RPT, a cycle; B) each value is a profile closed enum used by value by both modules (pattern of ADR-REG-005, ADR-DOC-002) and the type lives on the result port CHK declares. Recommended B: CHK masters the Check status (`AWAITING_DOCUMENTS`, `RUNNING`, `COMPLETED`, `FAILED`), the finding outcome (`SATISFIED`, `NOT_SATISFIED`, `UNDETERMINED`, ADR-CHK-002) and the Check failure reason (`TIMED_OUT`, `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`, `MODEL_OUTPUT_INVALID`, `MODEL_NOT_PERMITTED`, `UPLOAD_WINDOW_EXPIRED`, `INTERRUPTED`, `INTERNAL_ERROR`, ADR-CHK-005, ADR-CHK-006), and carries the profile's Overall Status (`COMPLIANT`, `NOT_COMPLIANT`, `NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW`) on the same port; RPT stores the codes it receives. A `COMPLETED` Check always carries an Overall Status; a `FAILED` Check never does and always carries a failure reason.

Reviewer challenge: the glossary puts Overall Status under RPT. Answer: the term and the stored value stay RPT's; only the definition of the closed list sits on CHK's port, as fetch mode sits in the profile and is carried by REG. Adding a value is a new CHK version. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §5, §7, §8, §9]; profile conventions.lookups; ADR-REG-002.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-002.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-002 — A finding is SATISFIED, NOT_SATISFIED or UNDETERMINED, and the Overall Status is NOT_COMPLIANT over NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW over COMPLIANT
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: cb2b65054bd0
traces      : POL-CHK-008, POL-CHK-013, POL-CHK-014, POL-CHK-015, POL-CHK-018

## Decision
The raw idea gives a finding "satisfied or not" and the report three overall statuses (§7), and says a missing or unreadable required document prevents COMPLIANT (G6). It does not say when NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW applies. An Overall Status of NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW needs a per-condition basis: a condition whose evidence could not be obtained is neither satisfied nor proven unsatisfied. Recommended: (a) a finding outcome is `SATISFIED`, `NOT_SATISFIED` or `UNDETERMINED`; UNDETERMINED when the evidence is not in the Check's data or documents (ADR-CHK-003), a document it needs is UNREADABLE, or the service query it needs was not read. (b) Each required document type is a condition: a MISSING document is NOT_SATISFIED; an UNREADABLE one is UNDETERMINED. (c) The Overall Status is `NOT_COMPLIANT` if any finding is NOT_SATISFIED; otherwise `NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW` if any finding is UNDETERMINED or any service query was not read; otherwise `COMPLIANT`. A definite failure outranks uncertainty because the request cannot become compliant by reviewing the uncertain part.

Alternative rejected: an unreadable required document gives NOT_COMPLIANT — it would tell the employee the applicant failed a condition when only the service failed to read the document; the employee can look at the document directly. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §7, §12]; domain-profile §5 G6, G10; ADR-DOC-002, ADR-DOC-007.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-003.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-003 — Explicit values and dates are decided in code from the model's stated value, comparison and limit, each verified against the Check's data and the service knowledge; unverifiable evidence makes the finding UNDETERMINED
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: ce74b474741d
traces      : POL-CHK-009, POL-CHK-013, POL-CHK-014

## Decision
The raw idea §5 step 5 runs "the deterministic checks in code: required documents present, explicit values and dates", but the REG contract supplies no declared check rules: the service package carries the service knowledge, queries, fetch mode, document source and required document types only (CON-REG-007). Options: A) defer explicit value and date checks until REG declares check types — contradicts the owner's §5 text for v1; B) require REG to add check declarations now — a REG change outside this module, against a published contract; C) let the model, which reads the service knowledge whole, state each explicit condition in its structured output as the value found, where it was found, the comparison and the limit, and let code (1) verify the value appears in the Check's query results or document content, (2) verify the limit appears in the service knowledge, and (3) recompute the comparison, overriding the model's outcome when they differ. Recommended C: the deterministic part of the outcome is computed by code, no REG change is needed, and evidence grounding (step 1) applies to every finding, not only explicit ones. A finding whose evidence or limit cannot be verified is UNDETERMINED (ADR-CHK-002). Required documents present and readable are decided in code directly from REG's required document types and DOC's outcomes.

Reviewer challenge: the model could still misread which limit applies. Answer: the limit must be literally present in the service knowledge, the comparison is recomputed, and the fixed known-result request set (POL-CHK-028) catches systematic misreadings on every model change. Declared check types remain a scope exception for a later REG version. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §4, §5, §7]; domain-profile §5 G10; CON-REG-007.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-004.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-004 — A manual-mode Check waits in AWAITING_DOCUMENTS until the employee confirms the uploads, bounded by an upload window of the platform configuration
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 99b578d19d90
traces      : POL-CHK-023, POL-CHK-024

## Decision
In `manual` fetch mode the employee uploads the documents for a Check that already exists (raw idea §8 `POST /checks/{id}/documents`), DOC binds each upload to the Check's identifier (ADR-DOC-003) and deletes them when the Check ends (ADR-DOC-008). Running the pipeline immediately would find no upload, report every required type MISSING, end the Check and delete any later upload. Recommended: a Check of a `manual` service is created with status `AWAITING_DOCUMENTS` and its pipeline does not run until the employee confirms, through INT, that the uploads are complete; it then becomes `RUNNING`, and the Check timeout counts from that point. The waiting time is bounded by an upload window held in the platform configuration beside the other per-Check limits (ADR-REG-006); when it elapses unconfirmed, the Check ends `FAILED` with reason `UPLOAD_WINDOW_EXPIRED` and DOC is told the Check ended. `path` and `blob` Checks start `RUNNING` directly.

Alternative rejected: run automatically after the first upload — the employee may have more files to add. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §6, §8]; ADR-DOC-003, ADR-DOC-008; ADR-REG-006.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-005.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-005 — Unread query data gives a report that cannot be COMPLIANT; a Check that cannot finish ends FAILED with a reason and no Overall Status; unfinished Checks are ended at start-up; every ending path notifies DOC
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: ce511ccdd355
traces      : POL-CHK-018, POL-CHK-019, POL-CHK-020, POL-CHK-021, POL-CHK-022

## Decision
G6 forbids skipping anything silently; G8 sets a timeout and maximum rows per Check; ADR-DOC-008 makes CHK notify DOC on every ending path. Recommended: (a) a service query that fails, or returns more rows than the maximum rows, is recorded in the report as not read — its data is never truncated silently — and the Check still produces a report that cannot be COMPLIANT (ADR-CHK-002), consistent with DOC's SOURCE_QUERY_FAILED rule (ADR-DOC-007). (b) A Check that cannot complete its pipeline ends `FAILED` with exactly one reason and no Overall Status: `TIMED_OUT` (Check timeout of the platform configuration), `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` (the comparison model cannot be reached), `MODEL_OUTPUT_INVALID` (the structured output does not fit the fixed report structure), `MODEL_NOT_PERMITTED` (ADR-CHK-006), `UPLOAD_WINDOW_EXPIRED` (ADR-CHK-004), `INTERRUPTED`, `INTERNAL_ERROR`. No partial overall status is stored. (c) At service start, CHK asks its result port for the Checks left `AWAITING_DOCUMENTS` or `RUNNING` by an earlier run and ends each one `FAILED` with reason `INTERRUPTED`; the result port therefore carries this read, implemented by RPT, keeping CHK free of any RPT dependency (ADR-REG-002). (d) Every ending path — COMPLETED or FAILED for any reason — sends DOC the end-of-Check notice (CON-DOC-005).

Alternative rejected: a timed-out Check stores a partial report as NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW — the employee could read an incomplete set of findings as the full picture. Resuming interrupted Checks is a scope exception. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §5, §12]; domain-profile §5 G6, G8, G9; ADR-DOC-007, ADR-DOC-008; ADR-REG-006.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-006.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-006 — The free-tier rule for the comparison model is enforced from the model's declared tier and the environment's data class, the same facts DOC uses
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: ff170b5ab40c
traces      : POL-CHK-027

## Decision
G13 lets a free-tier provider receive only synthetic or anonymised requests and documents, and the service cannot tell anonymised data from real data. DOC made its document-reading model testable with a declared model tier (FREE or APPROVED) and the environment's data class (SYNTHETIC or REAL) (ADR-DOC-009). Recommended: the comparison model configuration declares its tier, FREE or APPROVED (FREE when not declared), and CHK reads the same environment data class DOC reads (REAL when not declared). While the tier is FREE and the data class is REAL, CHK sends nothing to the comparison model and the Check ends `FAILED` with reason `MODEL_NOT_PERMITTED`. The go-live provider decision (D3) switches the tier to APPROVED by configuration alone; P1 places the data class setting so both modules read one value.

Alternative rejected: produce a report from the deterministic findings only — it would look like a complete report while the service knowledge was never compared. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §10]; domain-profile §5 G13, §8 D3; ADR-DOC-009.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-007.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-007 — Several Checks of the same request are allowed and fully independent; each Check uses the service package version current at its start for its whole life
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 8a6ea39e972c
traces      : POL-CHK-003, POL-CHK-025

## Decision
Amendment A1 shows the employee "the checks of a request", so a request may be checked more than once (for example after a document is corrected or a manual upload is redone). G9 forbids carrying data between Checks; G11 and ADR-REG-003 require the recorded version to identify what the Check used. Recommended: a new Check for a request that already has Checks is accepted and run independently — no result, data, model conversation or upload of an earlier Check is reused, and no Check is deduplicated or cancelled by a newer one. A Check takes the version that is current when it starts and keeps it until it ends, even if a newer version is loaded meanwhile. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §2, §4, §12, §15 A1]; domain-profile §5 G9, G11; ADR-REG-003.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-008.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-008 — Starting a Check needs an available service code, a request number and the employee's identity; CHK creates the Check run record through its result port and returns its identifier at once; an unavailable service is refused with no Check created
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 55cb93ff67f4
traces      : POL-CHK-001, POL-CHK-002

## Decision
The host sends the service code, the request number and the employee's identity (raw idea §5 step 1) and polls the status afterwards (§5, §8), so the Check needs an identifier before the pipeline runs, while the Check run record is RPT's (ADR-REG-001). Recommended: CHK checks the service code against REG (CON-REG-013); an unknown or withdrawn code — or a service whose connection is not activated (CON-REG-007) — is refused at once and no Check is created. Otherwise CHK creates the Check run record through its result port, which RPT implements and which returns the Check's identifier (`NUMBER(19)`, profile conventions.identifiers), returns that identifier to its caller and runs the pipeline in the background. The request number and the employee's identity are kept exactly as the host sent them, as strings, and are never foreign keys; the request number reaches the host only as the bound parameter of the service's queries (G4). The employee's identity is not authenticated in this version (A2).

Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §5, §8]; profile conventions.identifiers, conventions.lookups; CON-REG-007, CON-REG-013; ADR-REG-001, ADR-REG-002.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/CHK/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-009.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-009 — CHK states every raw-idea §12 guardrail at the surface it controls
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : Profile check AIAS-1 requires every guardrail of raw-idea §12 to be a REQ with at least one AC; the owner kept the §12 guardrails in this version (A2). CHK runs the service queries, calls the comparison model, decides the findings and the Overall Status, and ends every Check, so most guardrails act inside CHK. Guardrail 5 (storage root) and the maximum file size of guardrail 8 act inside DOC (ADR-DOC-005), guardrail 2's approval call inside INT. ADR-REG-008 left the Check-time enforcement to the module that runs it.
Decision    : Every §12 guardrail gets a CHK requirement at the surface CHK controls: (1) the LLM analyses only → no tool on the comparison model, nothing in its output executed (REQ-CHK-030, REQ-CHK-031); (2) approval only on the employee's action → CHK never calls the Approval API, never reads its definition, never records an Employee Decision (REQ-CHK-032, REQ-CHK-033); (3) read-only host access → service queries only through the platform MCP query channel over connections declared read-only (REQ-CHK-013, REQ-CHK-014); (4) bound parameters → the request number only as the bound input parameter, SQL text unaltered (REQ-CHK-011, REQ-CHK-012); (5) storage root → CHK opens no host file, BLOB column or upload itself, so DOC's storage-root check cannot be bypassed (REQ-CHK-018); (6) nothing skipped silently → unread queries and unread required documents appear in the report and block COMPLIANT (REQ-CHK-022, REQ-CHK-049, REQ-CHK-050); (7) content is data → REQ-CHK-034 … REQ-CHK-036; (8) per-Check limits → maximum rows and remaining time on every query call, Check timeout (REQ-CHK-016, REQ-CHK-051), maximum file size through DOC only (REQ-CHK-018); (9) nothing carried between Checks → REQ-CHK-063 … REQ-CHK-066.
Alternatives rejected: leaving guardrails 5 and 8 (file size) to DOC without a CHK requirement — CHK could then become a second way to documents.
Consequences: P3.1 review (AIAS-3..AIAS-8) scores the enforcement where it runs; INT still carries the Employee Decision path.
traces      : US-CHK-004, US-CHK-005, US-CHK-007, US-CHK-008, US-CHK-012, US-CHK-013, US-CHK-016, REQ-CHK-011, REQ-CHK-012, REQ-CHK-013, REQ-CHK-014, REQ-CHK-016, REQ-CHK-018, REQ-CHK-022, REQ-CHK-030, REQ-CHK-031, REQ-CHK-032, REQ-CHK-033, REQ-CHK-034, REQ-CHK-035, REQ-CHK-036, REQ-CHK-049, REQ-CHK-050, REQ-CHK-051, REQ-CHK-063, REQ-CHK-064, REQ-CHK-065, REQ-CHK-066

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-010.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-010 — The environment data class is one platform configuration value read by both DOC and CHK; the comparison model tier is part of CHK's comparison model configuration
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-CHK-006 enforces the free-tier rule (G13) from the comparison model's tier and the environment's data class, "the same facts DOC uses", and leaves P1 to place the data class so both modules read one value. ADR-DOC-009 introduced the data class (SYNTHETIC or REAL, REAL when not declared) as "the environment's document access setting". A second, CHK-owned data class setting could disagree with DOC's and let real data reach a free-tier model through one of the two paths.
Decision    : (1) The environment data class (SYNTHETIC or REAL; REAL when not declared) is ONE platform configuration value, held beside the per-Check limits of ADR-REG-006 (Check timeout, maximum rows, maximum file size, upload window), and read by both DOC and CHK; CHK defines no setting of its own for it. (2) The comparison model configuration declares the comparison model's provider, model identifier and tier (FREE or APPROVED; FREE when not declared); the tier is CHK's, separate from the document-reading model tier of ADR-DOC-009. (3) While the comparison model tier is FREE and the data class is REAL, CHK sends nothing to the comparison model and the Check ends FAILED with reason MODEL_NOT_PERMITTED (ADR-CHK-006). Neither value is an entity.
Alignment   : ADR-DOC-009's "environment's document access setting" names the same single value; DOC's behaviour is unchanged (non-breaking). DOC's next version should cite it as platform configuration (recorded for DOC); the platform configuration document of P3.1 lists it once.
Alternatives rejected: a CHK-owned data class setting — two values for one fact; deriving the data class from the comparison model tier — the tier says nothing about the data.
Consequences: Switching an environment to REAL data or a model to APPROVED is configuration only (D3). Tests set SYNTHETIC in the test environment.
traces      : US-CHK-018, POL-CHK-027, REQ-CHK-072, REQ-CHK-073, REQ-CHK-074, REQ-CHK-075

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-011.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-011 — The Check result port: the operations CHK declares and RPT implements; CHK owns no entity and keeps a Check's working data in memory only for that Check
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-REG-001 gives RPT the Check run record, the findings and the Check Document rows; ADR-REG-002 makes CHK declare a result port that RPT implements so the graph stays acyclic; ADR-CHK-005 adds a read of the unfinished Checks at start-up; ADR-CHK-004 needs a waiting manual Check to be found again when the employee confirms the uploads, and the upload window to be checked. CHK must still answer "which Check, which version, which status" without a table of its own.
Decision    : (1) CHK owns no stored entity and no table; it declares the Check result port with these operations, all carrying values only: create a Check run (service code, version number, fetch mode, request number, employee identity, initial status, start time) → Check identifier (`NUMBER(19)`, RPT's); mark a Check RUNNING; complete a Check (Overall Status, findings, document outcomes without content, unread service queries, metadata, end time); fail a Check (one failure reason, detail text, end time); read one Check (identifier, status, service code, version number, fetch mode, request number, employee identity, start time); list the unfinished Checks (status AWAITING_DOCUMENTS or RUNNING, with start time). (2) RPT implements the port in its own version; CHK never reads RPT tables. (3) A Check's working data — the loaded service package, query results, document outcomes and content, model input and output — lives in memory for the running Check only and is discarded when it ends (G9). (4) A manual Check resumed on confirmation reloads exactly its recorded version through REG's resolve operation (CON-REG-009).
Alternatives rejected: a CHK table of transient pipeline state — no requirement needs data to outlive a running Check, and a restart ends every unfinished Check anyway (ADR-CHK-005); RPT's interface called directly — adds CHK → RPT and closes a cycle (ADR-REG-002).
Consequences: P1.5 publishes the port in CHK's contract; RPT's analysis implements it (handed to RPT). P2 creates no CHK table.
traces      : US-CHK-001, US-CHK-011, US-CHK-013, US-CHK-014, US-CHK-016, REQ-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-044, REQ-CHK-046, REQ-CHK-047, REQ-CHK-054, REQ-CHK-055, REQ-CHK-057, REQ-CHK-060, REQ-CHK-063, REQ-CHK-065

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-012.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-012 — The known-result request set is a model-evaluation run delivered with the service; using a new model for real requests after it passes is the go-live gate, not a runtime lock
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : POL-CHK-028 and US-CHK-017 require the fixed set of test requests with known expected results to run on every comparison model change before the new model verifies real requests. The service cannot detect "a model change" across deployments without storing a model history, and the owner's D3 makes the real-data provider a go-live gate.
Decision    : (1) The known-result request set is a fixed set of synthetic requests, each with its service package and its expected Overall Status, delivered with the service and run by a model-evaluation run against the configured comparison model (profile test phase MODEL-EVAL, AIAS-10). (2) The run reports, per request, the expected and the reached Overall Status, and reports itself failed when any differs. (3) Running it on every comparison model change, and switching an environment to the new model for REAL data only after it passes, is the operational go-live gate of D3; the runtime adds no stored model history and no lock.
Alternatives rejected: a stored "last evaluated model" with a runtime block — adds a CHK table and an administration path the owner did not ask for (AIAS-2: no administration UI).
Consequences: The test stage derives the MODEL-EVAL cases from REQ-CHK-069 … REQ-CHK-071.
traces      : US-CHK-017, POL-CHK-028, REQ-CHK-069, REQ-CHK-070, REQ-CHK-071

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-013.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-013 — CHK's caller operations are in-process (start a Check, confirm uploads) with typed rejections INT maps to ProblemDetail; CHK exposes no HTTP operation at P1; a failed end-of-Check notice is retried once
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : INT owns the REST surface (domain-profile §4 row 5; raw idea §8). The host starts a Check and the employee confirms the uploads of a manual Check through INT (ADR-CHK-004, ADR-CHK-008). Starting a Check without caller authentication is accepted in this version (A2). ADR-DOC-008 requires the end-of-Check notice on every ending path, but says nothing about a notice that fails.
Decision    : (1) CHK offers INT two in-process operations of its injected interface: start a Check (service code, request number, employee identity) → Check identifier and status; confirm the uploads of a Check (Check identifier) → status RUNNING. Each refusal is a typed rejection with the exact message of its REQ or RULE; INT maps it to a ProblemDetail. (2) CHK exposes no HTTP operation in P1; if a later stage needs one, it follows ADR-DOC-011 (read-only, minimal). (3) If the end-of-Check notice to DOC fails, CHK keeps the Check's ending, sends the notice once more, and records a second failure in the service log; the Check's outcome never depends on the notice.
Alternatives rejected: CHK's own `POST /checks` — duplicates INT's endpoint; failing the Check when the notice fails — the report would be lost over a cleanup step.
Consequences: INT's analysis wires its start and upload-confirmation endpoints to these operations (handed to INT).
traces      : US-CHK-001, US-CHK-014, US-CHK-015, REQ-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-004, REQ-CHK-005, REQ-CHK-006, REQ-CHK-057, REQ-CHK-058, REQ-CHK-059, REQ-CHK-061, REQ-CHK-062

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-014.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-014 — A required document type is SATISFIED when at least one of its documents is READ; an unread service query is carried in the report as its query name with a detail text, with no new closed list
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-CHK-002 makes each required document type a condition (MISSING → NOT_SATISFIED, UNREADABLE → UNDETERMINED) but DOC may return more than one outcome for one type (ADR-DOC-006: several uploads per type; a host may list two files of one type). ADR-CHK-005 records an unread service query in the report but names no code list for why.
Decision    : (1) A required document type's finding is SATISFIED when at least one outcome of that type is READ; otherwise UNDETERMINED when at least one is UNREADABLE (evidence: the unreadable reason and detail); otherwise NOT_SATISFIED (MISSING). (2) An unread service query is handed to the result port as an entry with the query name and a detail text (the query error, or the row limit exceeded with the row count, or the connection refused by RULE-CHK-002); no closed list of query failure codes is introduced in v1, so ADR-CHK-001's lists are unchanged.
Alternatives rejected: requiring every document of a type to be READ — an extra unreadable copy would block an otherwise verified condition; a QUERY_FAILURE_REASON list — not asked for by any story, and a later version can add it.
Consequences: RPT stores the unread-query entries as text beside the report.
traces      : US-CHK-005, US-CHK-012, REQ-CHK-020, REQ-CHK-021, REQ-CHK-022, REQ-CHK-049, REQ-CHK-050, RULE-CHK-004

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-015.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-015 — CHK keeps one transient entity, the Active Check: one row per unfinished Check with its status and deadline, deleted when the Check ends; it makes every Check end exactly once
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-CHK-011 kept CHK free of any stored entity. The P2 database stage requires every module's script to carry a DB field traceability matrix with at least one column (factory block `dbf-matrix`, minItems 1, C0.1 CRITICAL), and every column needs an SRS entity origin — so the factory demands storage. Independently, the SRS has ending paths that can race on one Check: the timeout firing while the report is being stored, the upload window expiring while the employee confirms the uploads, start-up recovery. Nothing in CHK guarantees that exactly one ending reaches the result port and Document Access, and the deadlines exist only in memory. The owner allows CHK transient pipeline state where storage is demanded, provided CHK owns no report table (RPT owns the run, findings and Check Document rows — ADR-REG-001).
Decision    : (1) CHK owns ENT-CHK-001 Active Check (transactional, PRIVATE): activeCheckId (identity), checkId (the Check's identifier, RPT's, held as a value — never a foreign key), checkStatus (CHECK_STATUS, AWAITING_DOCUMENTS or RUNNING only), deadlineAt (end of the upload window or of the Check timeout), createdAt, updatedAt. (2) It is recorded when the Check run is created, updated on confirmation of the uploads, read for deadlines and for the confirmation check, and hard-deleted when the Check ends; whichever ending path deletes it ends the Check, and a path that finds it gone hands no further ending (REQ-CHK-076 … REQ-CHK-081; RULE-CHK-008 … RULE-CHK-010). (3) It holds no request data, result or document content (REQ-CHK-082, G9). (4) The start-up read of unfinished Checks stays on the result port (ADR-CHK-005; CON-CHK-011); the Active Checks of an earlier run are deleted after their Checks are ended INTERRUPTED. (5) This narrows ADR-CHK-011 point (1) from "no stored entity and no table" to "no report entity or table"; ADR-CHK-011 is otherwise unchanged.
Alternatives rejected: an invented column in a placeholder table — violates NO-COLUMN-INVENTION; the result port as the only arbiter of endings — needs a compare-and-set operation RPT has not designed, and would add an RPT round trip to every deadline check; storing pipeline working data — no requirement needs it and G9 forbids keeping it.
Consequences: P2 creates one CHK table; the contract is unchanged (the entity is PRIVATE and promised to no one). RPT's port stays as published.
traces      : US-CHK-013, US-CHK-014, US-CHK-016, ENT-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077, REQ-CHK-078, REQ-CHK-079, REQ-CHK-080, REQ-CHK-081, REQ-CHK-082, RULE-CHK-008, RULE-CHK-009, RULE-CHK-010

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-016.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-016 — CHK's closed lookups get no lookup table and no seed rows; CHECK_STATUS is checked on the Active Check column and all four lists on RPT's columns; the Check identifier is a value; the REG reads are SOFT-READ XMs
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P2        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : The P2 engine seeds every lookup key a module owns. CHK owns four closed lists (ADR-CHK-001); only CHECK_STATUS backs a CHK column (ENT-CHK-001.checkStatus, two of its values — RULE-CHK-009); the others travel by value through the Check result port to RPT. The platform has no lookup table (ADR-REG-010; DOC did the same, ADR-DOC-010). ENT-CHK-001.checkId is RPT's identifier.
Decision    : (1) No lookup table and no seed rows; CHK_ACTIVE_CHECK.CHECK_STATUS carries a CHECK of AWAITING_DOCUMENTS and RUNNING; RPT's script must carry CHECK constraints for OVERALL_STATUS, CHECK_STATUS, FINDING_OUTCOME and CHECK_FAILURE_REASON on the columns that store them (handed to RPT). (2) CHECK_ID is `NUMBER(19)` without FK and without XM — CHK never reads RPT tables; UNIQUE enforces RULE-CHK-008. (3) The SRS A8 entities ENT-REG-001 … ENT-REG-005 become XM-CHK-001 … XM-CHK-005, SOFT-READ, CONTRACTED against CON-REG-001 … CON-REG-005. (4) An index on DEADLINE_AT serves the deadline check (REQ-CHK-080).
Alternatives rejected: a CHK-owned lookup table — would be the platform's only one and duplicate code-level enums.
Consequences: P3.1 validates codes in the service layer against the enums.
traces      : ENT-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077, REQ-CHK-079, REQ-CHK-080, REQ-CHK-005, REQ-CHK-011, REQ-CHK-013, REQ-CHK-019

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-017.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-017 — CHK's one HTTP surface is a read-only view of a Check's Active Check (status and deadline); start, confirmation, pipeline and ending stay in-process service procedures; DOC is reached through its in-process interface without an XM edge
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P3.1      Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : The SRS gives CHK no HTTP operation (ADR-CHK-013): INT owns the REST surface and calls CHK in-process (CON-CHK-004, CON-CHK-005). The P3.1 engine derives every `API-*` block into `api-spec-chk.yaml`, the API document must carry at least one operation (C7.31, CRITICAL), every `QR-*` must be reached by an `API-*` (C7.22) and every REQ named by an `API-*` or a `DBF-*` (C7.6). DOC met the same constraint with one read-only endpoint (ADR-DOC-011). The owner asked CHK to keep any endpoint read-only and minimal. CHK's only stored data is the Active Check (ENT-CHK-001): status and deadline of an unfinished Check, no request data. DOC promises no entity and is consumed through its fetch and end-of-Check operations only (SRS A8), so the register holds no CHK → DOC XM.
Decision    : (1) CHK exposes exactly one HTTP operation, API-CHK-001 `GET /api/v1/active-checks/{checkId}`: the Active Check of one Check — checkId, checkStatus (AWAITING_DOCUMENTS or RUNNING), deadlineAt — so the embedded frontend (through INT or directly) can show the employee how long a `manual` Check waits for the uploads (US-CHK-014) or when a running Check times out; 404 when the Check is not unfinished (ended or unknown). It reads ENT-CHK-001 only and returns nothing of the request. (2) Start, upload confirmation, the pipeline, the deadline check, start-up recovery, the ending paths and the model-evaluation run are service-layer procedures of the SVC-API phase (pattern of ADR-DOC-011 and ADR-REG-011), each with its `Honours:` / `Declares:` line. (3) The QR catalog carries the query reached by API-CHK-001; the in-process insert, update, locking read and delete of CHK_ACTIVE_CHECK are written inline in their procedures. (4) API-CHK-001's `Covers` line names the requirements the in-process procedures implement, so the coverage check can see them. (5) DOC's fetch and end-of-Check operations are called through the injected Document Access interface in the PORTS phase; no XM is minted because DOC promises no entity and the CHK → DOC dependency is the platform edge (SRS A8).
Alternatives rejected: CHK's own `POST /checks` — duplicates INT's endpoint against ADR-CHK-013; exposing the result port over HTTP — RPT owns those records.
Consequences: The frontend and test stages bind only to API-CHK-001; pipeline test cases are derived from the ACs through the in-process `CheckEngine` interface. The endpoint is unauthenticated in this version like every other read (raw-idea A2).
traces      : US-CHK-014, ENT-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077, REQ-CHK-080, REQ-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-057

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-018.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-018 — HTTP errors are PLATFORM-STD rows of API-CHK-001 only; start and confirmation rejections are typed in-process exceptions with codes INT maps to ProblemDetail; Arabic messages pending
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P3.1      Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : The error catalog's rows must each be answered by an operation of `api-spec-chk.yaml` (C7.30). CHK's user-facing refusals (REQ-CHK-004, RULE-CHK-001, REQ-CHK-006, REQ-CHK-058, RULE-CHK-010) are raised by the in-process start and confirmation operations, which have no HTTP operation of their own (ADR-CHK-017); INT turns them into its HTTP responses. API-CHK-001 needs a not-found row, an identifier validation row and a server-failure row that no RULE carries. The SRS gives messages in English only; the profile lists en and ar.
Decision    : (1) The error catalog carries only the errors of API-CHK-001: `CHK-400-CHECK-ID-INVALID`, `CHK-404-ACTIVE-CHECK-NOT-FOUND`, `CHK-500`, all PLATFORM-STD. (2) The start and confirmation rejections are typed exceptions of the `CheckEngine` interface, each with a code in the profile format `{MOD}-{http}[-{SLUG}]` and the SRS message, listed in the plan's in-process rejection table: `CHK-400-START-INCOMPLETE` (REQ-CHK-004), `CHK-422-SERVICE-NOT-AVAILABLE` (RULE-CHK-001), `CHK-422-CONNECTION-NOT-ACTIVATED` (REQ-CHK-006), `CHK-404-CHECK-NOT-FOUND` (REQ-CHK-058), `CHK-409-CHECK-NOT-AWAITING-DOCUMENTS` (RULE-CHK-010). INT maps each to a ProblemDetail with that code and status. Pipeline outcomes (unread queries, UNDETERMINED findings, FAILED reasons) are results handed to the result port, never errors. (3) Arabic texts are `PENDING ADR-CHK-018` until the SRS carries them; nothing is translated here.
Consequences: INT's backend plan lists the five in-process codes in its own error catalog against its start and upload-confirmation endpoints (handed to INT).
traces      : REQ-CHK-004, REQ-CHK-005, REQ-CHK-006, REQ-CHK-058, REQ-CHK-059, RULE-CHK-001, RULE-CHK-010, ENT-CHK-001

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-019.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-019 — CHK's frontend plan carries no screen: 0 SCR and 0 UXD; F1–F3 state what the frontend consumes from API-CHK-001 (type, read hook, validator) without a SUB; F4 is empty; INT keeps every screen
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P3.2      Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : The P3.2 engine mints one `SCR-*` for each screen entry of the SRS, and `UXD-*` only when a screen of THIS module displays another module's data (§A.1, §A.5). CHK's SRS has no screen (PART B not applicable, SCR-REQ 0, module-registry AUTO-DECISION; [KB:raw-idea.md §15 A1]). The employee frontend (React + TypeScript, raw-idea amendment A1) belongs to INT, which holds all five screens (SCR-REQ-INT-001 … SCR-REQ-INT-005). The profile still gives the frontend exec plan four `sub_bearing` phases (F1–F4) and ALIGN-FE, each with one SUB per screen. With no screen, those phases have no SUB. Yet the `screen-hooks` block is mandatory in a frontend plan (C0.1), and its schema requires a screen id. CHK's one HTTP operation, API-CHK-001 `GET /api/v1/active-checks/{checkId}` (ADR-CHK-017), exists so that the embedded frontend can show the status and deadline of an unfinished Check. INT's SRS does not yet say that one of its screens shows that deadline. The split turns every phase without a SUB into one package (`plan_units`), so an empty phase becomes a package with no acceptance (C10.11 package-has-tests). Caller authentication is deferred (raw-idea A2), so there is no SEC-FE phase and no permission model.
Decision    : (1) No `SCR-*` and no `UXD-*` are minted. A UXD is keyed by the module that owns the screen (§A.5), so if one of INT's screens renders the Active Check, INT mints that UXD. The flow diagram has no FLOW block and the ui-ux-spec has no SCR block; each says so and gives the reason. (2) The frontend plan is bound to `api-spec-chk.yaml` (the `api-surface` block: mock = the document; REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077 and REQ-CHK-080 are bound to API-CHK-001; nothing is unmapped). Its F-phases state ONLY what a consumer of API-CHK-001 needs: F1 the `ActiveCheckView` / ProblemDetail types; F2 one read hook (`ACTIVE-CHECK-QUERY`) with cache key, error routing (404 `CHK-404-ACTIVE-CHECK-NOT-FOUND` = the Check is no longer unfinished, so the hook reports "ended" and shows no refusal text), loading and cache policy, with ONE `screen-hooks` block keyed to the consumer the read is offered to, INT's Check report screen entry SCR-REQ-INT-002 (which follows a Check until it ends). That block cites an existing screen entry and invents none; it sits in no SUB, so it decides nothing about INT's binding; F3 the runtime validators (the response schema and the checkId guard). There is no form, because CHK takes no input from the employee. Each phase has no SUB and traces to REQ/AC/API. (3) F4 (Screens & Routes) is empty: CHK registers no route, no lazy chunk and no component. Where the Active Check is shown is decided by INT's screens, which use the F2 hook. (4) The ALIGN-FE operations table lists API-CHK-001 with no CHK route. That row is marked ✗ under the template's rule and accepted by this ADR. (5) The test stage derives frontend TCs only for the packages that have content (F1, F2, F3), from the ACs that API-CHK-001 makes observable (AC-CHK-079, AC-CHK-080, AC-CHK-081, AC-CHK-083, AC-CHK-085). F4 has no TC because there is nothing it could accept. The resulting package-has-tests finding on F4 is a factory-level gap: no profile flag exempts an empty `sub_bearing` phase, and the split still emits it. The finding is recorded and left open rather than covered by an invented test case. The fix belongs to the profile/factory (`no_tests` on a screen-less module's F4, or a split that emits no package for a phase with no content).
Alternatives rejected: inventing a CHK "Check status" screen — this would duplicate INT's SCR-REQ-INT-002 and create a screen the SRS does not have (B1/B4 of the reconciliation); placing that `screen-hooks` block inside a SUB named after an INT screen — CHK would then claim another module's screen as one of its own packages; leaving F1–F3 empty as well — the only frontend fact CHK owns (the shape and error meaning of API-CHK-001) would then reach no implementer.
Consequences: INT's frontend plan may import the F2 hook for SCR-REQ-INT-002 / SCR-REQ-INT-003 (deadline of a `manual` Check) and invalidate the key `["active-check", checkId]` after its confirm-uploads mutation. That choice is INT's, and so is its UXD (handed to INT). The F4 package-has-tests finding stays open until the factory resolves it.
traces      : US-CHK-014, US-CHK-013, REQ-CHK-076, REQ-CHK-077, REQ-CHK-080, REQ-CHK-082, AC-CHK-079, AC-CHK-080, AC-CHK-081, AC-CHK-083, AC-CHK-085, ENT-CHK-001

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-020.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-020 — CHK test derivation: pipeline TCs exercise the in-process CheckEngine; REG-owned host data is created by REG's package load; the known-result set is six synthetic requests on one package; frontend TCs only for the packages F1–F3; no INT-UXD phase
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P4        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : The P4 engine binds every backend TC to an `API-*` and every frontend TC to an `SCR-*`. It creates any host-data catalogue value through "the call the owner of the lookup publishes for adding a value, by verb and path". It derives MODEL-EVAL cases (AIAS-10) from REQ-CHK-069 … REQ-CHK-071 (ADR-CHK-012). CHK has one HTTP operation, API-CHK-001 (ADR-CHK-017). The behaviour of 80 of its 82 REQs runs behind the in-process `CheckEngine` interface (start, confirmation, pipeline, ending, deadline check, start-up recovery, model-evaluation runner). CHK has no screen (ADR-CHK-019). The open lookups SERVICE_CODE and DOCUMENT_TYPE are REG's and are seeded with nothing. REG creates them only by loading package folders from its package directory at start-up (REQ-REG-005, REQ-REG-006, ADR-REG-007) and publishes no HTTP write (REQ-REG-017). The SRS gives every message in English only (ADR-CHK-018).
Decision    : (1) A backend TC whose AC is in-process behaviour names on its `Exercises` line the `CheckEngine` operation (or the scheduled / start-up procedure) it drives, with the contract item it honours, and cites API-CHK-001 only when the AC's outcome is observable there (AC-CHK-079 … AC-CHK-081, AC-CHK-083, AC-CHK-085). Ports the AC does not exercise (result port, Document Access, query channel, comparison model) are test doubles that record calls, as the backend plan states ("CHK's tests use an in-memory test double"). (2) The `Host data` line for SERVICE_CODE and DOCUMENT_TYPE values names the owner's creation path: REG's start-up load of the package folder `services/<code>/`, whose service definition lists the required document types. It also names REG's published read that shows the value exists, `GET /api/v1/services/{serviceCode}`. Host data is never moved into a CHK seed. No case uses an "inactive" state of a value. The unavailable service of AC-CHK-005 is REG's `available = false`, and the unknown code of AC-CHK-006 (`housing-grant`) is never created. So no two cases demand opposite states of one value. (3) Arabic messages are not asserted: every expected message carries `ar: PENDING ADR-CHK-018`. (4) The known-result request set is specified as six MODEL-EVAL TCs, one per synthetic request, plus the three AC-derived TCs of REQ-CHK-069 … REQ-CHK-071. The six requests use one synthetic service package (`scholarship-request` version 3 as delivered with the set: the condition "the GPA must be at least 3.0", required TRANSCRIPT and ID_CARD) and take their values from the ACs: 1 COMPLIANT, 3 NOT_COMPLIANT (failed GPA with an unreadable ID card — precedence; missing transcript; prompt injection in the transcript), 2 NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW (unreadable transcript; query over the row limit). The request numbers `SYN-001` … `SYN-006` are placeholders. The run uses environment data class SYNTHETIC. (5) Beyond one TC per AC, only these cases are added, each stated by a REQ or an AC limit: 2 BOUNDARY cases (exactly the maximum rows; the upload window not yet elapsed); 1 STATE case for the end-of-Check notice on each of the eight ending paths (REQ-CHK-061 "for any reason"); 1 ATTACK case for instruction-like text and a forged data delimiter in a query result (REQ-CHK-035, REQ-CHK-036). (6) Frontend TCs exist only for the frontend packages with content (F1, F2, F3) and drive the F2 hook against API-CHK-001 served by the mock. Five TCs fall under the TEST-PLAN-FE threshold (> 8), so the plan has no UI-FLOWS / INT-FLOW SUB. The INT-UXD phase is absent because CHK cites no UXD. The INT-XM phase holds one GRACEFUL-DEGRADATION TC per SOFT-READ edge XM-CHK-001 … XM-CHK-005 (five, under its threshold of > 8, so no SUB).
Alternatives rejected: binding every pipeline TC to API-CHK-001 — the endpoint cannot observe findings or the Overall Status, which are RPT's records; a CHK seed of `scholarship-request` — this would move host data into a product seed (C10.8).
Consequences: The F4 package keeps the package-has-tests gap recorded in ADR-CHK-019. The REG test double used in module scope must hold the same package that REG's load would produce.
traces      : REQ-CHK-061, REQ-CHK-069, REQ-CHK-070, REQ-CHK-071, REQ-CHK-035, REQ-CHK-036, REQ-CHK-050, REQ-CHK-060, AC-CHK-072, AC-CHK-073, AC-CHK-074, XM-CHK-001, XM-CHK-002, XM-CHK-003, XM-CHK-004, XM-CHK-005

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-CHK-021.md>>>
# ADR-CHK-021 — The empty F4 package is accepted by a structural TC (no CHK route, no write call, read only through the F2 hook); amends point (5) of ADR-CHK-019
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P4        Module: CHK        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-CHK-019 (5) left the F4 package (Screens & Routes, empty because CHK has no screen) without a TC and recorded the C10.11 package-has-tests finding as a factory-level gap. REG (TC-REG-071) and DOC (TC-DOC-074) faced the same empty F4 and resolved it inside their plans: each has a structural TC proving the module's frontend holds no route. That property is real and testable, and it is what F4 delivers for a module without screens.
Decision    : F4's acceptance is TC-CHK-106 (frontend-test-plan-chk.md, Package F4, derived from AC-CHK-081 / REQ-CHK-078 with API-CHK-001). It checks three things: CHK's frontend module registers 0 routes, 0 lazy chunks and 0 components; CHK's client sends only GET of API-CHK-001 and no write call; that read is issued only by ACTIVE-CHECK-QUERY (F2), and a completed Check reads as `ended`. ADR-CHK-019 is unchanged except that its point (5) — the open F4 gap needing a factory change — is superseded by this decision. No profile or factory change is needed.
Consequences: Every frontend package of CHK (F1–F4) has acceptance; C10.11 is cleared within the plan.
traces      : AC-CHK-081, REQ-CHK-078, ENT-CHK-001

<<<END ADR>>>
